One image build and one PP4 serving run at concurrency 232 on top of the round-8
configuration (9883.36 in 41073991312: 6241 requests, 121 TTFT and 3 ITL
failures, PP0 period 114.6/114.9/118.0 ms, job 11:21 of 17 min). Everything
round 8 pinned stays pinned. No profile pass is requested in this message.

What changed since round 8:
1. 8d26550 lets PP4 stage 0 admit a request while its Decode bootstrap is still
   running. The request takes a metadata buffer and waits in the pending list,
   as later stages already do; its KV is sent once the bootstrap completes.
   All 121 TTFT failures are in the 232-request burst at the start of the
   profiling phase. That burst drains at about 52 requests per second after a
   0.917 s intercept, and its first PP0 microbatch ran at +0.29 s with one
   request, because stage 0 waited for the Decode bootstrap. Expected: the
   burst starts 0.3-0.5 s earlier, about 15-25 more requests under 3 s,
   +0.25-0.4%.
2. aae4a24 (e2f0eb8 here) accounts the Prefill QKV/O DeepGEMM configs at the
   serving M of 7988. The stock 256x128 tile with A multicast is the L2 traffic
   minimum, so no config override.
3. 4bc07dc skips the three wholly padded 64-row waves of the M tail block (52
   valid rows of 256) in the Prefill QKV/O GEMMs: -2.34% of executed MMA and
   promotion work at M 7988. The stored bytes do not change. A new host patch
   selects it per call, only for the two Prefill shapes, BM 256, a partial M
   tile and the switch set. Stock and Decode keep their template text and
   normalized SASS. The image must be rebuilt for this host patch.
   - It has never served, so the job gates it on all twelve Prefill GPUs before
     model load. Each GPU checks byte equality with stock at M 7988 and 3994,
     runs 15 interleaved timing rounds and 200 endurance calls, and must save
     at least 2 us per QKV+O pair.
   - Any failure, timeout or late result keeps stock. The gate's step timeout
     is 110 s (fb46e31).
   - Expected 0.21-0.48 ms per period, +0.2-0.4%.

Expected: roughly 9,920-9,960 if both land, more if the burst gain is at the
top of its range. Job time about 11-12 min of 17.
